===== IPC HEADER =====
#pragma once
#include <QObject>
#include <QJsonObject>
#include <QJsonArray>
#include <QLocalServer>
#include <QPointer>
class WinuxCompositor;
class ShellIpcServer final:public QObject{
 Q_OBJECT
public:
 explicit ShellIpcServer(WinuxCompositor *compositor,QObject *parent=nullptr);
 bool start();
 void broadcast();
 QString socketPath()const{return m_path;}
private:
 void handleClient();
 void sendState(QLocalSocket *socket);
 void handleLine(QLocalSocket *socket,const QByteArray &line);
 WinuxCompositor *m_compositor;
 QLocalServer m_server;
 QList<QPointer<QLocalSocket>> m_clients;
 QString m_path;
};

===== IPC IMPLEMENTATION =====
#include "shell_ipc.h"
#include "compositor.h"
#include <QJsonDocument>
#include <QStandardPaths>
#include <QLocalSocket>
ShellIpcServer::ShellIpcServer(WinuxCompositor *c,QObject *p):QObject(p),m_compositor(c){connect(&m_server,&QLocalServer::newConnection,this,&ShellIpcServer::handleClient);connect(c,&WinuxCompositor::windowListChanged,this,&ShellIpcServer::broadcast);connect(c,&WinuxCompositor::workspaceChanged,this,&ShellIpcServer::broadcast);}
bool ShellIpcServer::start(){const QString runtime=QStandardPaths::writableLocation(QStandardPaths::RuntimeLocation);m_path=(runtime.isEmpty()?"/tmp":runtime)+"/winux11-shell.sock";QLocalServer::removeServer(m_path);m_server.setSocketOptions(QLocalServer::UserAccessOption);return m_server.listen(m_path);}
void ShellIpcServer::handleClient(){while(m_server.hasPendingConnections()){auto *s=m_server.nextPendingConnection();m_clients<<s;connect(s,&QLocalSocket::readyRead,this,[this,s]{while(s->canReadLine())handleLine(s,s->readLine());});connect(s,&QLocalSocket::disconnected,this,[this,s]{m_clients.removeAll(s);s->deleteLater();});sendState(s);}}
void ShellIpcServer::sendState(QLocalSocket *s){QJsonObject root;root["workspace"]=m_compositor->activeWorkspace();QJsonArray windows;for(const auto &v:m_compositor->windowList()){auto *o=v.value<QObject*>();auto *w=qobject_cast<WindowSurface*>(o);if(!w)continue;windows.append(QJsonObject{{"title",w->title()},{"workspace",w->workspace()},{"minimized",w->minimized()}});}root["windows"]=windows;s->write(QJsonDocument(root).toJson(QJsonDocument::Compact)+"\n");s->flush();}
void ShellIpcServer::broadcast(){for(const auto &ptr:m_clients){auto *s=ptr.data();if(s&&s->state()==QLocalSocket::ConnectedState)sendState(s);}}
void ShellIpcServer::handleLine(QLocalSocket *s,const QByteArray &line){Q_UNUSED(s);const auto p=QString::fromUtf8(line).trimmed().split(' ',Qt::KeepEmptyParts);if(p.isEmpty())return;if(p[0]=="activate"&&p.size()>1)m_compositor->activateIndex(p[1].toInt());else if(p[0]=="workspace"&&p.size()>1)m_compositor->setWorkspace(p[1].toInt());else if(p[0]=="launch"&&p.size()>1)m_compositor->launchApplication(p.mid(1).join(" "));else if(p[0]=="minimize")m_compositor->minimizeActive();else if(p[0]=="close")m_compositor->closeActive();else if(p[0]=="restore"&&p.size()>1)m_compositor->restoreWindow(p[1].toInt());else if(p[0]=="maximize")m_compositor->maximizeActive();else if(p[0]=="fullscreen")m_compositor->toggleFullscreenActive();else if(p[0]=="snap"&&p.size()>1)m_compositor->snapActive(p[1]);else if(p[0]=="move"&&p.size()>2)m_compositor->moveActive(p[1].toInt(),p[2].toInt());else if(p[0]=="resize"&&p.size()>2)m_compositor->resizeActive(p[1].toInt(),p[2].toInt());}

Worked for 26s
نتیجهٔ بررسی IPC خوب است: هر دو بخش Shell و کامپوزیتور از مسیر یکسان /run/user/1000/winux11-shell.sock استفاده می‌کنند. پس فعلاً لازم نیست مسیر سوکت IPC را تغییر بدهیم.

دو نکته هنوز باید بررسی شوند:

چرا سوکت Wayland قدیمی باقی می‌ماند و اسکریپت آن را با اتصال واقعی اشتباه می‌گیرد.
آیا Shell واقعاً می‌تواند به سوکت تازهٔ کامپوزیتور متصل شود و وضعیت اولیه را دریافت کند.
مرحلهٔ بعد: بررسی اسکریپت اجرا و سوکت‌های قدیمی
این دستور را اجرا کن تا قبل از اصلاح، منطق اجرای نشست را دقیق بررسی کنیم. فعلاً هیچ فایلی تغییر نمی‌کند و محیط گرافیکی Ubuntu دست‌نخورده می‌ماند.

===== SESSION SCRIPT =====
#!/bin/sh
set -eu

prefix="${WINUX11_PREFIX:-/usr}"
target_display="${WINUX11_WAYLAND_DISPLAY:-wayland-winux11}"
parent_wayland_display="${WAYLAND_DISPLAY:-}"
export XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=WINUX11
export XDG_SESSION_DESKTOP=WINUX11
export XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/run/user/$(id -u)}"
export PATH="$prefix/bin:$PATH"
# A software scene graph avoids EGL/3D initialization failures in VirtualBox
# and low-GPU environments. Users with a working accelerated stack can override
# this with WINUX11_QT_QUICK_BACKEND=opengl.
export QT_QUICK_BACKEND="${WINUX11_QT_QUICK_BACKEND:-software}"

if [ -n "${WINUX11_LOG_FILE:-}" ]; then
    log_file="$WINUX11_LOG_FILE"
    log_parent=$(dirname "$log_file")
    mkdir -p "$log_parent" 2>/dev/null || true
else
    state_home="${XDG_STATE_HOME:-${HOME:-/tmp}/.local/state}"
    log_dir="$state_home/winux11"
    if mkdir -p "$log_dir" 2>/dev/null; then
        log_file="$log_dir/session.log"
    else
        log_file="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/winux11-session-$(id -u).log"
    fi
fi
exec 3>&1 4>&2
exec >>"$log_file" 2>&1
log() { printf '%s [WINUX11] %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S' 2>/dev/null || printf 'time-unknown')" "$*"; }
fail() { log "ERROR: $*"; printf 'WINUX11: %s\nLog: %s\n' "$*" "$log_file" >&4; exit 1; }

[ -d "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR" ] || fail "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is unavailable: $XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"
[ -n "$target_display" ] || fail "WINUX11_WAYLAND_DISPLAY must not be empty"
case "$target_display" in */*) fail "WINUX11_WAYLAND_DISPLAY must be a socket name, not a path" ;; esac
compositor="$prefix/bin/winux11-compositor"
shell="$prefix/bin/winux11-shell"
[ -x "$compositor" ] || fail "compositor is not installed at $compositor"
[ -x "$shell" ] || fail "shell is not installed at $shell"

if [ -n "${WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QPA_PLATFORM:-}" ]; then
    compositor_qpa="$WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QPA_PLATFORM"
elif [ -n "${DISPLAY:-}" ]; then
    compositor_qpa=xcb
elif [ -n "$parent_wayland_display" ] && [ "$parent_wayland_display" != "$target_display" ]; then
    compositor_qpa=wayland
else
    compositor_qpa=eglfs
fi
log "Starting compositor with QPA=$compositor_qpa, QT_QUICK_BACKEND=$QT_QUICK_BACKEND, socket=$target_display, runtime=$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"

if [ "$compositor_qpa" = wayland ]; then
    [ -n "$parent_wayland_display" ] && [ "$parent_wayland_display" != "$target_display" ] || fail "Wayland backend needs a host WAYLAND_DISPLAY different from the WINUX11 socket"
    if [ -n "${WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND:-}" ]; then
        WAYLAND_DISPLAY="$parent_wayland_display" QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland QT_QUICK_BACKEND="$WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND" "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    else
        WAYLAND_DISPLAY="$parent_wayland_display" QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    fi
else
    if [ -n "${WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND:-}" ]; then
        QT_QPA_PLATFORM="$compositor_qpa" QT_QUICK_BACKEND="$WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND" "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    else
        QT_QPA_PLATFORM="$compositor_qpa" "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    fi
fi
compositor_pid=$!
cleanup() {
    if [ -n "${compositor_pid:-}" ]; then
        kill "$compositor_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "$compositor_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        compositor_pid=
    fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM

ready=0
for i in $(seq 1 300); do
    if [ -S "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/$target_display" ]; then ready=1; break; fi
    kill -0 "$compositor_pid" 2>/dev/null || fail "compositor exited before its Wayland socket became ready; inspect $log_file"
    sleep 0.05
done
[ "$ready" -eq 1 ] || fail "Wayland socket did not become ready within 15 seconds: $XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/$target_display"
log "Compositor socket is ready"

if [ "${WINUX11_HEADLESS_SMOKE:-0}" = 1 ]; then
    wait "$compositor_pid"
    compositor_pid=
    log "Headless smoke run completed"
    exit 0
fi

export WAYLAND_DISPLAY="$target_display"
export QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland
export XDG_SESSION_TYPE=wayland
log "Starting WINUX11 shell"
set +e
"$shell"
shell_status=$?
set -e
log "WINUX11 shell exited with status $shell_status"
exit "$shell_status"

===== GIT STATUS =====

===== INSTALLED SESSION LAUNCHER =====
/usr/bin/winux11-session
#!/bin/sh
set -eu

prefix="${WINUX11_PREFIX:-/usr}"
target_display="${WINUX11_WAYLAND_DISPLAY:-wayland-winux11}"
parent_wayland_display="${WAYLAND_DISPLAY:-}"
export XDG_CURRENT_DESKTOP=WINUX11
export XDG_SESSION_DESKTOP=WINUX11
export XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-/run/user/$(id -u)}"
export PATH="$prefix/bin:$PATH"
# A software scene graph avoids EGL/3D initialization failures in VirtualBox
# and low-GPU environments. Users with a working accelerated stack can override
# this with WINUX11_QT_QUICK_BACKEND=opengl.
export QT_QUICK_BACKEND="${WINUX11_QT_QUICK_BACKEND:-software}"

if [ -n "${WINUX11_LOG_FILE:-}" ]; then
    log_file="$WINUX11_LOG_FILE"
    log_parent=$(dirname "$log_file")
    mkdir -p "$log_parent" 2>/dev/null || true
else
    state_home="${XDG_STATE_HOME:-${HOME:-/tmp}/.local/state}"
    log_dir="$state_home/winux11"
    if mkdir -p "$log_dir" 2>/dev/null; then
        log_file="$log_dir/session.log"
    else
        log_file="${TMPDIR:-/tmp}/winux11-session-$(id -u).log"
    fi
fi
exec 3>&1 4>&2
exec >>"$log_file" 2>&1
log() { printf '%s [WINUX11] %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S' 2>/dev/null || printf 'time-unknown')" "$*"; }
fail() { log "ERROR: $*"; printf 'WINUX11: %s\nLog: %s\n' "$*" "$log_file" >&4; exit 1; }

[ -d "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR" ] || fail "XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is unavailable: $XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"
[ -n "$target_display" ] || fail "WINUX11_WAYLAND_DISPLAY must not be empty"
case "$target_display" in */*) fail "WINUX11_WAYLAND_DISPLAY must be a socket name, not a path" ;; esac
compositor="$prefix/bin/winux11-compositor"
shell="$prefix/bin/winux11-shell"
[ -x "$compositor" ] || fail "compositor is not installed at $compositor"
[ -x "$shell" ] || fail "shell is not installed at $shell"

if [ -n "${WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QPA_PLATFORM:-}" ]; then
    compositor_qpa="$WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QPA_PLATFORM"
elif [ -n "${DISPLAY:-}" ]; then
    compositor_qpa=xcb
elif [ -n "$parent_wayland_display" ] && [ "$parent_wayland_display" != "$target_display" ]; then
    compositor_qpa=wayland
else
    compositor_qpa=eglfs
fi
log "Starting compositor with QPA=$compositor_qpa, QT_QUICK_BACKEND=$QT_QUICK_BACKEND, socket=$target_display, runtime=$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"

if [ "$compositor_qpa" = wayland ]; then
    [ -n "$parent_wayland_display" ] && [ "$parent_wayland_display" != "$target_display" ] || fail "Wayland backend needs a host WAYLAND_DISPLAY different from the WINUX11 socket"
    if [ -n "${WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND:-}" ]; then
        WAYLAND_DISPLAY="$parent_wayland_display" QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland QT_QUICK_BACKEND="$WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND" "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    else
        WAYLAND_DISPLAY="$parent_wayland_display" QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    fi
else
    if [ -n "${WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND:-}" ]; then
        QT_QPA_PLATFORM="$compositor_qpa" QT_QUICK_BACKEND="$WINUX11_COMPOSITOR_QT_QUICK_BACKEND" "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    else
        QT_QPA_PLATFORM="$compositor_qpa" "$compositor" --wayland-socket-name="$target_display" &
    fi
fi
compositor_pid=$!
cleanup() {
    if [ -n "${compositor_pid:-}" ]; then
        kill "$compositor_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "$compositor_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        compositor_pid=
    fi
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM

ready=0
for i in $(seq 1 300); do
    if [ -S "$XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/$target_display" ]; then ready=1; break; fi
    kill -0 "$compositor_pid" 2>/dev/null || fail "compositor exited before its Wayland socket became ready; inspect $log_file"
    sleep 0.05
done
[ "$ready" -eq 1 ] || fail "Wayland socket did not become ready within 15 seconds: $XDG_RUNTIME_DIR/$target_display"
log "Compositor socket is ready"

if [ "${WINUX11_HEADLESS_SMOKE:-0}" = 1 ]; then
    wait "$compositor_pid"
    compositor_pid=
    log "Headless smoke run completed"
    exit 0
fi

export WAYLAND_DISPLAY="$target_display"
export QT_QPA_PLATFORM=wayland
export XDG_SESSION_TYPE=wayland
log "Starting WINUX11 shell"
set +e
"$shell"
shell_status=$?
set -e
log "WINUX11 shell exited with status $shell_status"
exit "$shell_status"
